## 3. Code and scripts — required production responsibilities + last-known inspection targets

### 3.1 Required production component and script map

Repository discovery decides the exact paths, but the following responsibilities are mandatory first-class production components. Reuse a proven existing module when it truly owns the responsibility; otherwise create a clearly named component. The requirement matrix records the final path/function.

| Responsibility | Required production owner | Required script/CLI owner | Minimum proof |
|---|---|---|---|
| Raw webhook ingress/auth/persist-before-2xx | webhook route + raw ingress service | fixture/replay command | TEST-006/007 + integration artifact |
| FIDS population acquisition/range/DST/retry | provider client + FIDS census service | `v39:gate5:population`, frame inputs | TEST-001–004 |
| Canonical flight identity/codeshare/retime | identity service | identity fixture runner | TEST-008 |
| OOOI/milestone/T mapping | milestone normalizer | Gate-0.5 pilot analyzer | TEST-009 |
| PRE snapshots | snapshot builder | offline materializer/replay | TEST-011/013/014 |
| AIRBORNE points/trajectory/snapshots | airborne event + trajectory pipeline | cadence/trajectory diagnostics | TEST-012 + cadence cases |
| Outcome terminalization/recovery | outcome service + recovery scheduler | recovery fixture/runner | TEST-015 |
| `m_i`, zero-yield, coverage floor | sampling/adaptation controller | deterministic replay | TEST-016 |
| Calendar/crossover solver | scheduler/solver | `v39:calendar:solve` | TEST-017 |
| Anchor probe scoring/censoring/caps | probe controller | `v39:probe:stage1/2` | TEST-018 |
| Credit/accounting/settlement/watchdog | ledger + watchdog | Gate-3/4/status commands | TEST-019–021 |
| Security/retention/expiry | retention/security service | `v39:security:verify` | TEST-023 |
| History/weather as-known joins | feature store + weather service | offline replay | TEST-013/014 |
| Protected split/evaluation freeze | split/manifest service | `v39:freeze` | TEST-025/026 plus TEST-022/023/027 for budget/security/manifest completeness |
| Traceability/registry/dictionary/lineage | audit metadata | preflight checks | TEST-027 |
| Contradiction/regression scanner | scanner module | `v39:scanner` | TEST-027 seeded bad fixtures + TEST-028 full-suite/build/replay evidence |

Scripts are not secondary utilities. Each script must call the same production services used by the real runtime, or explicitly state that it is a read-only verifier. A script that reimplements business rules separately is a P1 divergence risk and cannot close a gate.

### 3.2 Last-known repository code walkthrough — active inspection aid, not current status proof

> **Repository-verification rule:** the concrete paths/function names below came from the previously observed repository and are useful inspection targets. They become current only if §1 intake verifies them on the present commit. Paid CLI examples in this walkthrough are explanatory legacy syntax; use §2 for current authorized commands.
>
> The full plain-English walkthrough of every file is in
> **`AugMDnotes/CODE_WALKTHROUGH.md`** — read that for the complete tour. This
> section summarizes the pieces that matter for what we're doing right now (the
> probe), so you can follow a run.

#### 3.2.1 `scripts/anchor_probe.ts` — the probe runner (`npm run anchor-probe`)

**Historical command plumbing (verification target, not current repository fact).** In the last-known repository snapshot, `package.json` mapped the legacy `anchor-probe` alias to `tsx scripts/anchor_probe.ts`, the legacy `canary` alias to `tsx scripts/credit_canary.ts`, and `build-catalog` to `tsx scripts/build_stratified_catalog.ts`. The `--` convention passes later arguments through npm to the underlying script. **Do not assume any of those aliases, files, or mappings still exist.** Current repository intake (§1) must inspect `package.json`/the actual launcher and then map or implement the canonical §2 `v39:`* command surface. Operators use only the verified canonical interfaces after that mapping is evidenced.

**Last-known legacy intent versus current requirement.** This script was built around an older probe design. It is an inspection target, **not** current executable authority. The current binding owner is Plan §§4.5 and 9 plus the pre-probe freeze record: Stage 1 is target-2h and Stage 2 target-4h, capacity is `rows_per_hour≥60`, `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500` is cumulative per immutable `probe_budget_day_id`, weights are 0.4/0.2/0.2/0.2, and the shortlist is exactly **12 dual-eligible HUB-tier candidates across regions** including WSSS and OMAA. The Plan does **not** impose a fixed per-region quota on the 12-candidate shortlist. Exogenous components use the frozen §4.5 traffic/geo/carrier formulas and source hashes; probe outcomes supply only the observed-yield 20%. The repository agent must correct/verify the script against those rules before mapping it to `v39:probe:stage1/2`.

Its modes:

- `--stage 1 [--icao X] [--hours N]` — **historical CLI syntax only** for a target-2h cap-censored Stage-1 probe. Current operator interface is `v39:probe:stage1` under an exact authorization; the runner may block for the bounded run, but actual exposure/stop/censoring/settlement must be recorded.
- `--stage 2` — **historical CLI syntax only** for target-4h cap-censored confirmation; current interface is `v39:probe:stage2` and must refuse any airport without a valid completed Stage-1 result
  (**required refusal behavior, not a present-code claim**): the current implementation must make the rl8 out-of-order Stage-2 start impossible, and TEST-015/related probe-state tests must prove that refusal before paid use.
- `--score` — **historical CLI mode only**. A current scorer must implement the frozen Plan §§4.5/9 formulas, the `rows_per_hour≥60` capacity gate, the frozen yield-reference rules, deterministic tie handling, and proposed exactly-five lock only after valid Stage-2 confirmation. The legacy scorer is an inspection target until repository evidence proves equivalence.
- `--status` — list recorded probes from `clean.adb_anchor_probe`.
- `--cleanup [--force]` — **historical CLI description only**. Current cleanup must be scoped to experiment-owned subscriptions and must preserve an audit record. A generic `--force` safety bypass is forbidden. A foreign/untracked active billable subscription is a refusal condition. Binding R1 requires it be disabled/deleted before the experiment can proceed, but that cleanup must be ownership-aware, explicitly authorized/audited, and never implemented as a generic force bypass; `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false` does **not** make broad deletion safe.
- `--check-webhook` — prints the public webhook URL, whether
  `REPLIT_DOMAINS`/`WEBHOOK_BASE_URL` are set, and does a GET reachability probe.

**Current mechanics required before this script may be mapped to production:** resolve and validate the exact AUTH record → reserve against the immutable `probe_budget_day_id` cumulative `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500` **and** pre-run/floor ledgers → R1 exclusivity/owned-subscription check → verify the current feed/cost/tier under the frozen provider contract → create only the authorized owned subscription with `maxDeliveryRetries=0` → persist the run/probe ownership record → execute the target window until normal end or cap censoring → delete/inactivate and verify owned cleanup → apply the **shared settlement rule** (frozen initial wait/poll/stability window; ≥3 consecutive equal authoritative balance reads; change resets; timeout blocks) → compute settled external Alert spend from `B_before−B_stable` and reconcile to immutable SEND/item accounting → derive canonical confirmed/ambiguous flight-instance and chain metrics plus complete-bucket stability → write the external report/evidence record and only then mark the probe terminal. Any legacy 1,900-only probe guard, fixed 10-second settle, single post-read, free-feed assumption, or legacy dedup/chain SQL is insufficient and must be replaced or proven equivalent.

#### 3.2.2 `scripts/credit_canary.ts` — Gate 3 (`npm run canary` **(legacy alias; current mapped interface: `v39:gate3:canary`)**)

A **canary** = a tiny controlled live test meant to die loudly before the real work. Its cost is **not guaranteed to be 1 credit**: billing is per flight item per provider SEND. Freeze a very small authorized Alert-credit reservation/ceiling, then measure actual settled spend. The historical rl9 attempt happened to cost 1 credit. The official canary:

1. Reads the live balance (`balance_before`).
2. **Asserts R1 exclusivity** — lists subscriptions, refuses to continue if any
   foreign ACTIVE billable subscription exists (the rl8 orphans would have failed
   this).
3. Creates exactly one **authorized** owned subscription for the resolved Gate-3 airport/filter/window with `maxDeliveryRetries: 0`; no hard-coded KLAX requirement comes from this walkthrough.
4. Runs only for the frozen authorized duration, deletes/inactivates the owned subscription, then applies the shared settlement procedure; fixed 120 s / 5 s waits are historical implementation details, not current settlement proof.
5. Applies the shared settlement rule: after the configured initial wait, poll the authoritative balance until at least **three consecutive equal reads** (or a larger frozen count) span the required stability window; any change resets the count; timeout = `SETTLEMENT_UNRESOLVED`. Then `C_external = balance_before − balance_stable`.
6. Reconciles against the current immutable delivery/item/attempt ledger mapped during repository intake: `C_internal = Σ notification_items` for the owned subscription/SEND scope, plus raw persistence, processing, rows, and failures. Historical `adb_ingest_events` is only a candidate audit source and cannot substitute for the normalized immutable raw/item ownership proof.
7. **Official Gate-3 PASS iff `C_external == C_internal` (`CANARY_TOLERANCE=0`), the shared ≥3-read settlement rule passes, raw-before-2xx evidence passes, `delivery_failures=0`, >0 items arrived, owned cleanup is confirmed, and no foreign billable subscription exists.** Otherwise FAIL/STOP.

This is the command that FAILED on 08-19: `C_external=1`, `C_internal=0`,
`delivery_failures=1` — the webhook was reachable (charged 1 credit) but the
handler threw before storing. A later `is_randomized` code change was reported as the historical bug fix, but that does **not** prove a current canary will pass; current presence/wiring/tests and live reconciliation must be re-established.

#### 3.2.3 `scripts/build_stratified_catalog.ts` — step 11 (`npm run build-catalog`)

Builds the sampling frame from the measured universe (current Plan §4 + §17). The last-known script called provider coverage inputs that were documented as non-paid/free-tier at the time; **current tier/cost/version must be re-verified under Gate 1**. Historically it then
historically classified the 276 curated airports and coerced every other coverage airport into REGIONAL with `traffic_prior=1.0`. **That legacy behavior is noncompliant with binding Plan §4.1**: airports missing the frozen traffic reference must remain `tier='UNCLASSIFIED'`, `tier_verified=false`, `tier_source='missing_reference'` and stay out of primary tier-stratified sampling until deterministically classified. Region assignment must use the frozen country→macro-region mapping, not ICAO-prefix heuristics except where the Plan explicitly defines a deterministic fallback/override. Per-feed eligibility is still computed independently. The historical "267 curated + 4,053 unclassified→REGIONAL" and "18/18 strata" outputs are **provisional evidence only**. Under the current §§16–17 sequence, Gate 1 first remeasures coverage; only then are the traffic/region rules frozen and the final frame rebuilt/hashed before the pre-probe freeze, safety smoke, and Gate 2.

#### 3.2.4 `server/lib/disruption/adbCollectionController_v3.ts` — the brain

This legacy controller is **not yet evidence of binding Phase-6 behavior**. Binding configuration must distinguish the `HARD_CAP=1,900` Alert-credit ceiling per immutable Phase-6 `budget_day_id`, the frozen `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling≤57,900`, the protected Alert floor, and the separate seven-category REST/API-unit ledger; it must not treat a generic 1,900 "budget" or an old fixed retry allowance as interchangeable resources. The tier mix remains `{HUB:1, MID:2, REGIONAL:1}`, with the enabled anchor consuming the HUB slot, and the six UTC slots are `{0,4,8,12,16,20}`. `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false` is mandatory until the authorized Phase-6 start transition. HUB/MID selection follows Plan §8.5. REGIONAL selection must be wired to the seeded adaptive rule **within the already selected tier×region cell**: `adaptive_score_i=traffic_prior_i·m_i`, `draw_score_i=adaptive_score_i·coverage_boost_i`, and `p_i=draw_score_i/Σdraw_score` over the current eligible set, with Phase-6 initialization `m_i=1`, EMA=NULL, the frozen recurrence/zero-yield state machine, persistent coverage-floor eligibility, and replayable seed. Any legacy uniform `1/n_eligible` production draw is compliant **only on an initialization step where all eligible scores are equal**, not as the general algorithm. Batch start/stop must use the frozen template plus SEND-aware accounting/reconciliation; current repository wiring remains to be proven by implementation tests.

#### 3.2.5 The webhook path (`routes_v3.ts` + `flightNotificationExtractor_v3.ts` + `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts`)

**The data flow, answered in plain words** (the "what comes from us vs AeroDataBox"
question):

1. AeroDataBox sees a flight change for a subscribed airport and **POSTs a payload**
   to `POST /api/v1/webhooks/aerodatabox[/secret]`. The payload contains the
   `subscription` block, the `balance` block, and a `flights[]` array.
2. Our route performs authentication/minimal envelope validation and looks up sampling metadata by `subscription_id`. It **must not promise to always 2xx**: failure to durably commit the normalized raw envelope/item layer before acknowledgement returns non-2xx so silent loss cannot be disguised as success. After durable raw commit, semantic parse/normalization failures are recorded as processing attempts and may be acknowledged according to the frozen retry policy. `maxDeliveryRetries=0` is the intended subscription setting, but protocol correctness is not weakened merely to avoid a possible paid retry.
3. `flightNotificationExtractor_v3.ts` may flatten provider items into convenience/current-state columns, but the binding implementation must first preserve raw delivery/item identities and all canonical provider/source clocks from Plan §6; derived `data_stage`/sampling metadata are OUR annotations, not provider facts.
4. Any legacy mutable `flight_data_pre_post` upsert by `dedup_key` is a **current-state convenience path only**. It must not replace or deduplicate immutable `raw_delivery`/`raw_delivery_item` or the canonical semantic-observation identity; `(flight, carrier, locReportedUtc)` is invalid for non-location updates and is not a universal event key.
5. Durable raw persistence occurs **before successful 2xx** according to the Plan's receive→commit raw→2xx→semantic-processing contract. `adb_ingest_events`/processing attempts are audit/accounting records; implementation evidence must prove the actual route follows this order.

**The rl9 bug, and the fix (in this same file):** migration 0022 created
`is_randomized` as `NOT NULL DEFAULT false`. But for any delivery with no managed
batch (probe/canary subscription), the extractor set `isRandomized: null` — and
inserting NULL into a NOT NULL column makes Postgres throw, which made the whole
handler throw, store 0 rows, and record `delivery_failure=1` (your canary FAIL). A later code change reportedly defaulted unmanaged rows to `isRandomized: false`, consistent with the NOT NULL constraint for non-randomized unmanaged probe/canary rows. Treat that as **historical fix provenance only**: current code existence, production wiring, raw-before-2xx ordering, TEST-006/007/019, and any new canary/probe behavior remain NOT_VERIFIED.

#### 3.2.6 `aerodataboxLimiter_v3.ts` — the AeroDataBox client

Throttled/rate-limited REST calls: `getBalance`, `refillBalance`,
`createSubscription`/`listSubscriptions`/`deleteSubscription`/`getSubscription`,
`checkAirportFeeds`, `listFeedAirports`, `defaultWebhookUrl` were last-known client methods. The current production client/caller must be verified to create every experiment-owned subscription with `maxDeliveryRetries: 0`; **billing remains one Alert credit per flight item on provider SEND, not one credit per HTTP delivery**. Webhook URL/port requirements must be taken from the pinned provider contract/live Gate-0.5 evidence; a historical `:443` workaround is not treated as universal provider-contract authority.

#### 3.2.7 Historical typecheck baseline — not grandfathered

A historical repository snapshot reported **57 pre-existing type errors** (mostly in `server/routes.ts`). That number is provenance, **not a passing criterion**. Phase-6 technical readiness requires the repository's binding typecheck command to exit 0 (or an explicitly adjudicated toolchain exception recorded as a blocker, not silently grandfathered). The next repository-capable pass must run the command and record its exact exit code/output; "no new errors relative to 57" is insufficient for GO.

#### 3.2.8 Current required formulas versus last-known legacy code

The binding formulas come from the **current Plan**, not from whatever arithmetic happens to exist in a legacy script. Repository intake must map each row below to production code and tests; any different legacy formula is a correction blocker.

| Requirement | Current binding formula/rule | Legacy inspection status |
|---|---|---|
| Unique flights per credit | `uf_per_credit = confirmed_unique_flight_instances / settled_alert_credits`; also report the ambiguity-sensitive upper bound when identity is not fully resolved | Legacy row/dedup counting must be audited against canonical `flight_instance_id` and settled SEND-side credit scope |
| Chain links per credit | confirmed compatible chain links under canonical tail/flight identity divided by settled Alert credits | Legacy `count(*)-1 GROUP BY aircraft_reg` is only a candidate and cannot be accepted until identity/continuity rules are proven |
| Stability | `1/(1+CV)` on **complete observed 15-minute buckets only**, with frozen minimum bucket rule; incomplete/censored buckets do not silently enter | Legacy bucket code must be checked for completeness/censoring handling |
| Component standardization | per component, `clamp(probe/reference,0,1)` against WSSS primary; OMAA only under the pre-frozen component fallback rule; neither valid ⇒ `NOT_SCORABLE` | Legacy blanket fallback logic must be compared to the frozen per-component rule |
| Yield score | arithmetic mean of the three valid standardized yield components required by Plan §9.2 | No imputation from invalid references |
| Capacity gate | `rows_per_hour ≥ 60`, separate feasibility gate and never part of anchor score | Must remain separate from scoring |
| Traffic score | `traffic_score = min(1, traffic_metric_value / hub_cut_metric)` using the frozen §4.1 metric/boundary | Any legacy “flights/year ÷ shortlist maximum” formula is **noncompliant** |
| Geo score | `0.5*degree_norm + 0.3*intl_share + 0.2*region_rarity`, with `region_rarity=1-region_share` and frozen normalization inputs | Legacy monolithic `geo index` must be replaced or proven algebraically identical to the frozen inputs |
| Carrier score | `0.6*effective_carriers_norm + 0.4*intl_share` with frozen normalization inputs | Legacy generic `carrier index` must be replaced or proven identical |
| Anchor score | `0.40*traffic_score + 0.20*geo_score + 0.20*carrier_score + 0.20*yield_score` | Weights are binding; inputs must be the current formulas above |
| External Alert spend | `C_external = B_before - B_stable` **after** the shared stable-read settlement procedure | A single `balance_after` read is insufficient |
| Internal Alert spend | `C_internal = Σ notification_items` over the immutable owned delivery/item SEND scope | Mixed legacy audit tables alone are insufficient without raw/item ownership/provenance |

**Status:** these formulas are document-closed but **repository implementation is NOT_VERIFIED**. Do not describe the legacy `computeScores` implementation as “the frozen code” until repository mapping, TEST-008/018/019, and the pre-probe freeze prove it.

#### 3.2.9 How to read a probe run — required evidence shape versus historical output

A historical Stage-1 runner printed a simple sequence such as balance → subscription → waiting → delete → one post-balance → row/flight/chain metrics. That is useful for understanding the old script, but it is **not sufficient current gate evidence**.

A current mapped `v39:probe:stage1/2` run must additionally emit/reference: exact AUTH ID and resolved candidate/window; `probe_budget_day_id`; before-run reservations against the 500-credit probe cap and pre-run/floor ledgers; provider contract/tier/version; owned subscription identity; start/end/censoring reason; cleanup confirmation; the complete authoritative settlement-read series and `B_stable`; immutable internal SEND/item accounting; `C_external/C_internal` reconciliation; canonical confirmed/ambiguous flight-instance bounds; chain denominator/identity QC; complete-bucket stability and minimum-bucket status; capacity result; source/reference/preprobe hashes; exit status; report/evidence ID and artifact hash. `SKIPPED`, `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`, `MISMATCH`, cleanup failure, unresolved settlement, or foreign subscription all stop advancement according to §1/§6.

Do not “walk away” based only on a historical console line: the runner itself must own timeout/cleanup/settlement/fail-closed behavior and leave a recoverable evidence record even if the shell disconnects.

---
<a id="sec-4"></a>
## 4. Phase-by-phase roadmap and gate state machine

```text
DOCUMENT-AUDITED
  → REPOSITORY INTAKE
  → OFFLINE COUNTERS EXACT ZERO
  → Gate 0
  → PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION
  → Gate 1
  → pre-probe reference freeze
  → specifically authorized safety smoke
  → Gate 2 Stage 1/2 (each covered by explicit authorization)
  → Gate 3
  → Gate 0.5
  → Gate 4
  → Gate 5
  → history/weather/retention/calendar/manifest readiness
  → FREEZE
  → separate explicit Phase-6 authorization
  → controller may enable auto-collect for the frozen run only
```

Stop at the first missing/failed/insufficient item. A later gate cannot PASS
out of order. The safety smoke is not Gate 3. Gate 0.5 is after Gate 3 and does
not authorize Phase 6.

### 4.1 Gate evidence record — operational summary

The **single detailed gate-evidence schema lives in §20.1** and the actual report lives in `V3.9_RUN_REPORTS_AND_EVIDENCE.md`. Every gate must use that schema; §4 does not duplicate it. Operationally, no gate can advance without: identity/hashes, exact authorization where required, predecessor evidence, bounded inputs/budgets, owned-subscription lifecycle, execution/exit codes, data/accounting/settlement evidence, one terminal result (`PASS` / `FAIL` / `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE` / `BLOCKED`), artifact hashes, and the exact next permitted action.

### 4.2 Human authorization is a data record, not an implication

Before every paid/mutating run, create `AUTH-YYYYMMDD-###` naming:

```text
authorized operation and gate
account/subscription channel
exact airport(s), query/filter, and window/duration
maximum Alert credits and REST units by category
start-not-before and expires-at UTC
owned-subscription prefix/tag and cleanup owner
required stop conditions
approver identity/time
Plan/preprobe/manifest hash that is authorized
```

No authorization may say “whatever is needed,” auto-extend a window, permit
unbounded retries, borrow categories, or authorize Phase 6 before FREEZE.

### 4.3 Paid-run refusal checks

Immediately before any SEND/mutation, atomically re-check:

1. prior states/gates still PASS on the current commit/config/provider pin;
2. authorization exists, matches exact run, and has not expired;
3. auto-collect remains false unless this is the final authorized Phase-6
   transition;
4. no foreign billable subscription can affect accounting;
5. all intended subscriptions are owned/tagged to this run;
6. retention/security record is current and expiry worker healthy;
7. authoritative balance/account reads and both ledgers reconcile;
8. new reservation plus worst-unsettled-burst margin fits the operation,
   pre-run/day/run ceilings, protected floor, and REST category cap;
9. webhook TLS, provider-supported auth or its approved compensating control,
   raw persistence, and the stop worker are healthy;
10. settlement/cleanup timers and a human stop owner are active.

Any false/unknown check returns `REFUSED_BEFORE_SEND` and records why.

### 4.4 Paid command invocation contract — §2 is the single command authority

The exact operator command templates are defined **once, in §2.5–§2.6**. This phase section intentionally does not repeat them. The rule here is only: later-stage commands are bounded interfaces to implement/verify; every external/paid/mutating invocation must resolve an exact plan, match an unexpired authorization record, print the bounded action/ceilings/stop conditions before SEND, and fail nonzero on any mismatch. No `--force`, wildcard airport, unbounded duration, or automatic continuation bypass is allowed.

### 4.5 Detailed phase-by-phase walkthrough (active operational explanation)

> **Execution note for this detailed walkthrough:** statuses and concrete filenames/`npm run ...` examples below are explanatory/last-known repository targets unless §1/§2 re-establishes them on the current commit. The only commands an operator may execute are the current mapped command surface in §2 under the fail-closed authorization state machine. Historical `Actual:` lines remain dated evidence, not present PASS state.


> The plan's runbook (§17) is organized as **phases with steps inside them** — we
> follow the same structure here (the old "steps with numbers only" framing was
> confusing and has been retired; current operational content is consolidated in §§1–4, while superseded command/history forms are retained only in §36).

| Phase | Name | What it is | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Offline implementation closure | Make all Phase-6-critical production paths safe, budget-protected and leakage-safe; run offline tests/migrations/typecheck. No paid collection. | **NOT CLOSED — legacy foundation exists, but corrected FIDS/raw ingress/identity/history-weather/snapshots/terminalizer/m_i/calendar/budget wiring remains UNKNOWN_GT_ZERO until repo evidence closes it** |
| 1 | Gate 0 | Verify active 60,000-unit entitlement/cycle state, current refill/account rules, and the exact Alert + seven-category API-unit budget identities. | **BLOCKED — offline Gate-0 code/test closure plus fresh account/cycle evidence still required; historical refill/balance evidence is provenance only** |
| 2 | Gates 1–2 | Remeasure coverage, rebuild the verified traffic/region frame, execute the frozen cap-censored anchor protocol, then lock five valid Stage-2-confirmed candidates. | **BLOCKED — historical coverage/frame artifacts exist but are provisional/noncompliant with the binding frame rule; corrected frame/probe path and required evidence are not closed** |
| 3 | Gate 3 → Gate 0.5 → Gate 4 | Official canary, dedicated provider/payload/timing pilot, then scaled-cap/reconciliation proof. | **BLOCKED** |
| 4 | Gate 5 | Census validation (FIDS population vs webhook events). | **BLOCKED** |
| 5 | FREEZE | Versioned manifest, hash split-assignment rule, config frozen. | **BLOCKED / NOT FROZEN** |
| 6 | The 31-batch-day run | 31 solver-generated parent batch-days; `HARD_CAP=1,900` applies to each immutable Phase-6 `budget_day_id` (one parent batch-day), not to a provider/UTC calendar day. Total spend is separately bounded by the frozen `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling≤57,900` and may be lower; no next budget day starts until the prior one is settled. | **NO-GO / NOT STARTED** |
| 7 | Month-1 deliverables | Execute/rerun snapshot builders that **must have been production-wired and offline-tested before Phase 6**, then leakage-safe evaluation of Model −1 vs Model 1 and cost/sample curves. | **BLOCKED_BY_PHASE6** |

**Key rule:** Phase 6 waits for the complete current sequence: offline closure → Gate 0 → PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION → Gate 1 → pre-probe freeze → authorized safety smoke → Gate 2 → Gate 3 → Gate 0.5 → Gate 4 → Gate 5 → history/weather/retention/calendar/manifest readiness → FREEZE → separate explicit Phase-6 authorization.

#### 4.5.1 Phase 0 — Offline implementation closure (historical foundation was observed; current production paths remain NOT VERIFIED until repository intake)

Plan §17's offline phase requires every Phase-6-critical production path and test to be closed before paid gates: R1 exclusivity, SEND-aware budget protection, exact canary reconciliation, failure PAUSE behavior, frozen/randomized templates, manifest refusal behavior, FIDS population ingestion, immutable raw ingress, identity-v2, history/weather as-of joins, PRE/AIRBORNE snapshot builders, target-specific terminalization, adaptive `m_i`, calendar solver, and the exact budget/registry/matrix checks. Legacy R1–R7/S-layer foundations and migrations are historical evidence only. **They do not prove the corrected binding paths are production-wired. Current Phase 0 is NOT CLOSED until the repository-dependent counters in §0 and §7 reach exact evidence-backed zero.**

#### 4.5.2 Phase 1 — Gate 0 (BLOCKED pending offline closure + current live account evidence)

Historical evidence includes a 2026-08-16 refill/conversion observation and a then-current balance, but those values are **not current Gate-0 PASS evidence**. Gate 0 now requires: (1) offline budget/report/limiter tests complete; (2) confirmation that the USER-CONFIRMED `MONTHLY_PLAN_ENTITLEMENT_UNITS=60000` plan is still active; (3) current billing-cycle dates, units consumed/remaining and settled Alert balance; (4) current refill/account limits/conversion evidence; and (5) reconciled Alert-credit and seven-category API-unit identities. The final Phase-5 manifest is written later, after all gates and pre-run freezes; Gate 0 records sourced budget evidence rather than prematurely “committing the manifest.”

#### 4.5.3 Phase 2 — Gates 1–2 (historical progress exists; current execution BLOCKED until offline/reference prerequisites)

- **Step 10 — coverage:** a dated 2026-08-17 run measured a provider-backed universe through the legacy local aggregator. **Historical evidence only.** Current Gate 1 must remeasure from the pinned provider coverage inputs/local aggregator, record source/version/timestamp/hash/cost, and must not target the old 4,332 count.
- **Step 11 — frame:** the 2026-08-18 builder produced the legacy 4,320-row artifact by coercing missing-reference airports into REGIONAL and using an ICAO-prefix region heuristic. **Historical/provisional and noncompliant with binding §§4.1–4.2.** Rebuild with the frozen traffic reference, explicit `UNCLASSIFIED`, frozen country→region mapping/overrides, explicit `UNMAPPED`, and the final eligibility/hash contract.
- **Step 12 — anchor probe:** legacy script/probe evidence exists, including a zero-delivery attempt. The corrected cap-censored Stage-1/Stage-2 protocol, replacement logic, pre-probe freeze record, safety smoke, and offline tests must be closed before a new paid probe. Gate 2 is therefore **not passed**.

#### 4.5.4 Phase 3 — Gate 3 → Gate 0.5 → Gate 4

Steps 13–16 cover R1 cleanup, the **official** isolated credit canary, Gate-0.5 provider/payload/timing measurement, and Gate-4 cap/reliability proof. A historical Gate-3 canary attempt failed and is provenance only. The required exact-`tol=0` canary correction, scaled offline Gate-4 tests and all live re-verification remain **NOT PASSED**.

#### 4.5.5 Phase 4 — Gate 5 (census validation; BLOCKED)

Steps 17–19 validate the role-aware, append-only FIDS population observations over exact service intervals, guarded cutoff availability, independent webhook capture, snapshot expectations/creation, target outcomes and external US spot checks where available. A standalone FIDS helper was present in the last-observed repository, but current existence and the corrected production caller/schema selector/boundary guard are not proven; Gate 5 is **NOT PASSED**.

#### 4.5.6 Phase 5 — FREEZE (BLOCKED)

Steps 20–21 write the final versioned manifest only after all required gates/reference freezes/readiness artifacts pass. It must include the complete canonical registry, frame/reference hashes, exact calendar/budget, builder/code versions, account-budget evidence, retention classification/status and `split_rule_hash` (not future row IDs). The protected row IDs are materialized and hashed only after Phase 6 under the frozen rule. **Manifest is NOT FROZEN.**

#### 4.5.7 Phase 6 — The 31-batch-day run (NO-GO)

Steps 22–24: the solver-generated calendar (26 × 4h + 3 × 2×2h + 2 × up-to-6h = 31 ordered batch-days; washout may span more than 31 calendar dates), daily watchdog enforcing
1,900 cap / SOFT_STOP / exclusivity / delivery-failure pause, weekly diagnostics,
monthly airborne cadence re-measurement. **Not started — `autoCollect=false`.**

#### 4.5.8 Phase 7 — Month-1 deliverables (BLOCKED_BY_PHASE6)

Steps 25–29: execute/rerun the PRE/AIRBORNE snapshot builders that had to be production-wired and offline-tested before Phase 6, materialize actual rows, then perform leakage-safe Model −1 vs Model 1 evaluation, cost/sample curves, collection-mechanism ablation and POST pilot. Labeled "early operational pilot", never "validated production model".

### 4.6 Detailed Phase 0–7 implementation sheets (A30 16-field format)

#### 4.6.1 Phase 0 — Code deltas — A30 full sheet (16 fields)

- **Objective:** Make code safe, budget-protected, scientifically valid (R1-R7, S1-S5) without spending.
- **Prerequisites:** binding V3.9-f.8 PART 1 available, `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false`; document architecture locked but manifest not frozen.
- **Inputs:** PART1 §15, A30 §§37-78.
- **Steps:** R1 exclusivity, send-aware watchdog, exact canary, template/manifest; S1 population; S2 normalized raw layers; S3 event-log-first; S4 provenance; S5 versioned semantic identities supporting location and non-location observations.
- **Code files:** `server/lib/disruption/adbCollectionController_v3.ts:80` config, `:516` draw, `:639` startBatch, `:843` stopBatch, `:1404` watchdog; `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts:139` upsert, `:201` research key; `flightNotificationExtractor_v3.ts:242`; `flightInstanceCanonical_v3.ts:1` (new f.7), `fidsCensus_v3.ts:1` stub, `historicalFeatureStore_v3.ts:1` stub.
- **DB tables:** `adb_collection_batches`, `adb_ingest_events`, `flight_events`, `raw_airborne_events`, `clean_airborne_points`, `flight_trajectory`, `flight_airborne_snapshots`, `flight_population`, `historical_feature_store` (stub).
- **Migrations:** historical observations included 0017–0025, but current inventory is unknown. Repository intake must map the binding raw/semantic contract to the current migration IDs (historically 0024/0025 or renamed successors) and prove fresh-install + upgrade from the actual prior target + repeat/idempotency + constraint/append-only behavior before any current status claim.
- **Config:** binding constants include `ADB_BATCH_BUDGET=1900`, protected floor `1000`, and official canary `CANARY_TOLERANCE=0`; the runtime SOFT_STOP margin is **TBD/MEASURE→FREEZE** (historical `50` is only the Plan-required arithmetic/test fixture until valid evidence freezes the applicable margin). `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false` during closure.
- **Commands:** repository-equivalent typecheck/lint/build/test/migration validation plus offline catalog/registry/scanner checks. Historical “57 errors” is provenance only; readiness requires the binding typecheck to exit 0 or remain an explicit blocker.
- **API/provider:** none (offline).
- **Outputs:** Migrated DB, controller safe mode, frame table empty until Gate1.
- **Artifacts:** migration SHAs, `shared/schema.ts` types (drift noted).
- **Tests:** migration idempotency (0022 re-run), extractor real payload, `available_at` NOT NULL check pending.
- **Gate:** none, but must pass `grep maxDeliveryRetries=0` + `sampling_weight NULL`.
- **Failure:** missing `available_at` wiring, `payload_sha256` NULL → FAIL Gate 0.5.
- **Rollback:** `down` migration not used; re-run `applyBootMigrations()` idempotent.
- **DoD correction:** legacy foundation status does not close Sep1 requirements. FIDS, identity-v2 wiring, history/weather tables, snapshots, terminalizer, raw ingress, and adaptive `m_i` remain document requirements with last-known partial/code evidence only; current implementation status stays NOT_VERIFIED until production callers and integration tests are proven on the current commit.
- **Next-phase dependency:** repository intake must identify the actual migrations carrying the binding raw/semantic contract (the audit historically named 0024/0025 or explicitly renamed successors). Every mapped migration must pass fresh-install, upgrade from the actual prior target, constraint/append-only, and repeat/idempotency validation; required target application evidence then precedes any affected live gate. File existence alone is not PASS.

#### 4.6.2 Phase 1 — Gate 0 — Budget partition

- **Objective:** Verify money math before measuring world.
- **Prereq:** current **offline P0/P1 implementation closure PASS**, then human authorization and RapidAPI credentials live. Historical Phase-0 foundation alone is insufficient.
- **Inputs:** USER-CONFIRMED `MONTHLY_PLAN_ENTITLEMENT_UNITS=60000`, current account/plan/cycle evidence, and credentials needed for authorized reads.
- **Steps 5-9:** record the **current subscribed plan/marketplace account**, billing-cycle entitlement and balance; verify current refill conversion/caps with the minimum explicitly authorized live action required by the provider/account; re-verify and freeze the current FIDS endpoint tier/unit cost/range plus the generated seven-category REST budget; use the mapped `v39:gate0:inspect` interface and record sourced evidence. Any prior public tier/cost pin is dated evidence, not an execution constant. Historical refill/balance measurements are provenance, not present-day PASS criteria.
- **Code:** historical `scripts/gate0_budget_report.ts` / `aerodataboxLimiter_v3.ts` are inspection targets only; repository intake must map the actual current account/budget/provider client owners.
- **Tables:** Gate-0 evidence belongs in the current evidence/budget registry and later manifest inputs; historical `adb_collection_meta` / `adb_ingest_events` are inspection targets, not proof of the current canonical ledger.
- **Config:** `MONTHLY_PLAN_ENTITLEMENT_UNITS=60000` (user-confirmed), account-plan identity/evidence, `ADB_RESERVE_CREDITS`, and separate per-cycle usage fields.
- **Commands:** current mapped `v39:gate0:inspect -- --auth <AUTH_ID> --evidence-id <GATE0_ID>`; provider/account reads must be explicit in the resolved plan, including a verified zero ceiling when free.
- **API:** RapidAPI/account quota + Flight-Alert balance; the historical account observation found 1 refill unit→1 credit, but Gate 0 must re-verify the current conversion/caps.
- **Outputs:** a new Gate-0 evidence artifact and hash for later manifest inclusion; the final manifest is **not** written/frozen at Gate 0.
- **Artifacts:** new external Gate-0 report/evidence record + hashes; historical `rl*.md` remains dated provenance only.
- **Tests/evidence:** historical rl3 1-credit refill proved 1 unit→1 credit at that time; current Gate 0 re-verifies conversion/caps from authoritative account/provider evidence and uses the minimum explicitly authorized test action only if needed.
- **Gate:** Gate 0 PASS iff the user-confirmed 60,000 entitlement is confirmed unchanged for the active subscribed plan, current cycle state is sourced, refill/caps are verified, and both Alert/API-unit trees reconcile before any billable FIDS call.
- **Failure:** wrong `ADB_PLAN` or 58,900 vs 57,900 confusion → MISMATCH.
- **Recovery:** re-check account, no code fix.
- **DoD:** a newly sourced Gate-0 artifact records current settled Alert balance, account/plan/billing-cycle/API entitlement, authorized refill evidence as required, protected floor, pre-run allocation, exact computed `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling`, `unallocated_alert_credits`, and the seven-category REST ledger; draft manifest uses binding Plan `V3.9-f.8`. Historical balance `2900/2901` is not a current PASS criterion.
- **Next:** `PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION` must PASS after Gate 0; only then may current Gate 1 begin.

#### 4.6.3 Phase 2 — Gates 1–2 — Coverage + frame + anchors

- **Objective:** Measure universe, build measured frame, probe anchors.
- **Prereq:** Gate 0 PASS and `PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION=PASS` for the Gate-1/reference content path.
- **Steps 10-12:** acquire current provider coverage through the authorization-guarded, repository-verified Gate-1 interface and record current endpoint/tier/cost/version/hash; freeze/hash the licensed traffic/region/reference rules and normalization inputs; rebuild/hash the final frame with missing-reference airports retained `UNCLASSIFIED`; **then** write/hash the complete `preprobe_reference_freeze_record` embedding the source/rule artifact and final-frame hash plus the exact shortlist/replacements/yield-reference/probe protocol; run the authorized safety smoke only after that record is complete, and only afterward execute the cap-censored Stage-1/Stage-2 anchor protocol. Historical `4332` universe / `4320` frame / `18/18` outputs are provenance, not targets.
- **Code:** historical coverage/catalog/probe paths are inspection targets only. Repository intake must map the current coverage acquisition, reference/frame builder, probe/scoring, ledger/settlement, and owned-subscription services before any command is considered implemented.
- **Tables:** `clean.adb_sampling_frame` (tier_source, traffic_prior, region, pre/post_eligible, in_frame), `adb_anchor_probe`.
- **Config:** `traffic_source_version`, `region_mapping_version`, `W_EXOGENOUS=0.4` etc., `CAPACITY_GATE=60`, `STAGE1_HOURS=2`.
- **Commands:** `v39:gate1:coverage -- --auth <AUTH_ID> ...` → `v39:reference:freeze` → `v39:frame:build` → `v39:preprobe:freeze` → specifically authorized `v39:smoke:safety` → `v39:probe:stage1/2`, with stop/evidence checks between stages. Historical aliases are inspection targets only.
- **API:** provider health/coverage inputs documented as non-paid/FREE-TIER in the Sep-2 source audit feed the measured universe only after current tier/cost/version re-verification; a repository `/api/v1/collection/coverage` is only a local aggregator. Paid probe notifications cost 1 Alert credit per **flight item on provider SEND**, even if delivery fails.
- **Outputs:** current frozen frame/stratum artifact (with any `UNCLASSIFIED` explicitly reported), plus cap-censored probe exposure, `rows/h`, canonical-flight-instance yield/credit, chain-links/credit, stability, capacity and replacement trace. Historical `18/18` is not a required current numerical target.
- **Artifacts:** hash-stable current `adb_sampling_frame`/reference artifacts with measured row count (not hard-coded `4320`) and append probe records including actual exposure/censoring/replacements.
- **Tests:** deterministic tier/region mapping and hashes; every airport maps to exactly one verified region or explicit `UNMAPPED`; missing traffic stays `UNCLASSIFIED`; report all 18 possible verified tier×region cell counts without requiring historical `18/18`; eligibility invariants; cap-censoring/capacity/replacement rules.
- **Gate:** Gate 1 requires a complete contract-pinned current coverage artifact and reference readiness; diagnostics such as universe/frame/catalog relationships are reported but no historical count is a target. Gate 2 uses the frozen current formulas, separate capacity gate, complete replacement trace, and five valid Stage-2 confirmations before lock.
- **Failure:** any raw-ingress/accounting safety prerequisite or mandatory tiny pre-probe smoke fails → **no paid Stage-1 probe**. The official Gate-3 canary is not a Gate-2 prerequisite; it occurs after completed Gate 2 under the binding sequence.
- **Recovery:** stop, reconcile, and use only a repository-mapped **owned-subscription** cleanup/status path with explicit scope; no generic force bypass and no deletion of foreign subscriptions by convenience flag.
- **DoD:** current coverage/frame sources and hashes verified under binding f.8 rules; missing-reference airports are not silently coerced to REGIONAL; pre-probe freeze record is immutable; all required anchor candidates are cap-censored/confirmed under the frozen protocol. Historical coverage/probe evidence cannot substitute for this current freeze.
- **Next:** Phase 3 needs Gates1-2.

#### 4.6.4 Phase 3 — Gate 3 → Gate 0.5 → Gate 4 — canary + dedicated pilot + reliability

- **Objective:** Prove whole accounting + webhook path honestly.
- **Prereq:** Gate 2 PASS, prior paid stage fully settled/closed, exact Gate-3 authorization, and `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false` outside the bounded authorized command.
- **Steps 13-16:** official Gate-3 canary uses the shared ≥3-read settlement rule and exact `C_external==C_internal` with tolerance 0; Gate 0.5 is a separate adequately sampled pilot; Gate 4 proves the cap/failure state machine offline first and then performs only a small authorized live reconciliation/second-start check. The runtime soft-stop margin is frozen from valid evidence, not assumed from an old 1,850 line.
- **Code:** `scripts/credit_canary.ts:36`, `server/routes_v3.ts:81` webhook, `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts:254` dual insert, `server/lib/disruption/adbCollectionController_v3.ts:1404` watchdog.
- **Tables:** `adb_collection_batches` balance_before/after, `adb_ingest_events`.
- **Config:** `maxDeliveryRetries=0`; `ADB_DAILY_SOFT_STOP_MARGIN=50`/1,850 is the pre-measure or arithmetic fixture unless valid smoke/canary evidence freezes that margin for the applicable run.
- **Commands:** current mapped `v39:gate3:canary` → `v39:gate05:pilot` → `v39:gate4:live-check`, each under its exact authorization where mutating/live.
- **API:** 1 credit per item SEND.
- **Outputs:** `reconciliation_status PASS/MISMATCH`, `delivery_failures`.
- **Artifacts:** historical `rl9.md` records the dated FAIL and reported fix provenance; a new current Gate-3 report is required and no PASS is pending/assumed.
- **Tests:** canary composition + settle + orphan cleanup.
- **Gates:** Gate 3 exact reconciliation + shared stable settlement; Gate 0.5 PASS only when all frozen adequacy minimums and semantic/durability/leakage checks pass (`INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE` is not PASS); Gate 4 scaled-offline state machine + bounded live reconciliation, retries 0.
- **Failure:** `delivery_failure=1` (rl9 is_randomized bug) → PAUSE+flag.
- **Recovery:** preserve the historical fix as provenance; for the current run, stop, re-run repository tests/preflight, obtain a fresh exact authorization, then use `v39:gate3:canary`.
- **DoD:** LIVE-VERIFIED canary PASS + Gate0.5 cadence measured (currently BLOCKED).
- **Next:** Phase4 needs Gates3/0.5/4.

#### 4.6.5 Phase 4 — Gate 5 — Census validation

- **Objective:** Validate the provider-observable population and quantify independent capture/snapshot/outcome dimensions. Only `captured_in_population ≤ population_total` is an invariant; outside-population captures are reported separately.
- **Prereq:** Gates3/0.5 PASS (payload honest).
- **Steps 17-19:** For sample airport service intervals, fetch FIDS `direction=Both` using the live account's verified max range and recursive splitting when needed; build population, then report captured-in/outside, expected/created snapshots, and observed/missing outcomes. FAA/BTS is US-subset external validation only.
- **Code:** standalone `server/lib/disruption/fidsCensus_v3.ts` exposes `fetchFidsPopulation` + `utcIntervalToLocal`, and an identity helper exists; focused evidence does **not** prove the guarded population scheduler/role-aware selector/identity-v2 path is production-wired.
- **Tables:** `flight_population` (source_type fids/schedule, observed_via_webhook), `flight_snapshots`, `flight_outcomes`.
- **Config:** `withCancelled=true`, `withCodeshared=true` with explicit ambiguity status, `withCargo=false`, `withPrivate=false`; exact half-open service interval converted with airport IANA timezone and split to the live-verified account max range. Unit cost remains provisional until verified.
- **Current command:** `v39:gate5:population -- --auth <AUTH_ID> --evidence-id <GATE5_ID>` after §2 mapping and authorization. Historical `npm run fids-census -- --sample` is only a repository inspection target and cannot authorize a live/API-unit action.
- **API:** verified AeroDataBox airport-FIDS path `GET /flights/airports/{codeType}/{code}/{fromLocal}/{toLocal}` with the binding `direction=Both`/scope parameters; **current account unit cost and maximum range are Gate-0/Gate-0.5 evidence and must not be hard-coded from an old public observation**.
- **Outputs:** funnel proportions per airport/tier.
- **Artifacts:** `flight_population` rows hash.
- **Tests:** DST spring-forward/fall-back, duplicate handling, schedule revision preservation.
- **Gate:** Gate5 reports role-aware `population_total`, `captured_in_population`, `captured_outside_population`, expected/created snapshots, missing features, and observed/missing outcomes. Only `captured_in_population <= population_total`; outside captures are investigated and never forced into the denominator.
- **Failure:** webhook capture rate implausibly low → provider coverage finding.
- **Recovery:** regenerate/re-freeze the affected seven-category API-unit budget only through the binding pre-run change-control path; never rely on the superseded “<1000 REST” shorthand or borrow silently across categories.
- **DoD:** Gate-5 live funnel evidence PASS **after** the guarded FIDS production path and required offline tests are proven; current state is NOT PASSED (standalone helper exists, production wiring/evidence incomplete).
- **Next:** Phase5 FREEZE.

#### 4.6.6 Phase 5 — FREEZE — manifest + split rule

- **Objective:** Freeze everything that must not adapt on Phase6 outcomes.
- **Prereq:** Gates0-5 PASS (or Gates0.5/5 measure→freeze values frozen).
- **Steps 20-21:** Write `adb_collection_meta` versioned manifest (frame hash, tier_version, `region_mapping_version`, `anchor_score version 40/20/20/20`, `scheduler seed`, `anchor_seed`, `adaptive_version`, `FIDS version`, `milestone mapping`, `cadence thresholds`, `grace`, `snapshot builder SHA`, `history_ready_at`, `split_rule_version/hash` — but NOT row IDs), then AFTER Phase6 apply the rule to actual rows and hash test row IDs read-only (Plan §13.2). A30 §73 correction + A31 §24 correction.
- **Code:** `server/lib/disruption/adbCollectionController_v3.ts:291` `writeManifest`/`readManifest`.
- **Tables:** `adb_collection_meta` key='manifest', split/test-set metadata.
- **Config:** every current Plan-required frozen/MEASURE→FREEZE value plus the unchanged `preprobe_reference_freeze_record`, exact ledgers, retention/security/history/weather readiness, calendar and split hashes.
- **Current command contract:** `v39:calendar:solve` (when calendar inputs are ready) then `v39:freeze -- --evidence-id <FREEZE_ID>` after every prerequisite passes. Split-rule materialization/hash behavior is part of the verified freeze/evaluation implementation; old `write-manifest/hash-split-rule` aliases are only repository inspection targets.
- **Outputs:** `manifest.json` SHA, `split_rule_hash`.
- **Tests:** `v39:scanner` + `v39:preflight` must PASS against current Plan/Log/repository with seeded bad fixtures and exact-zero unresolved P0/P1/repository counters; raw lexical grep is diagnostic only.
- **Gate:** FREEZE PASS iff the complete V3.9-f.8 manifest embeds all required evidence/hashes (including unchanged preprobe freeze, exact budgets, calendar, registry, retention/readiness, split-rule hash) and every predecessor is PASS.
- **Failure:** missing traffic_source_hash → NOT FROZEN.
- **DoD:** FROZEN (currently NOT FROZEN).
- **Next:** Phase6.

#### 4.6.7 Phase 6 — 31-batch-day run — 26×4h + 3×2×2h + 2×up-to-6h (≈84/10/6 by batch-day shape)

- **Objective:** Run the preregistered **31-batch-day** experiment within the frozen `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling ≤ 57,900`; 57,900 is a maximum design ceiling, never a spending target, and washout may span more than 31 calendar dates.
- **Prereq:** FREEZE PASS.
- **Steps 22-24:** Calendar 26×4h+3×2×2h+2×up-to-6h (see Plan §17); watchdog polling cadence is **frozen before paid use** and the old fixed 60-second tick is not accepted unless offline tests/pre-run evidence prove it safe. Enforce 1,900 budget-day ceiling, frozen soft-stop/unsettled-burst controls, exclusivity, delivery_failure→pause/flag; run weekly diagnostics (including coverage-age ≤5 d core) and monthly cadence re-measurement.
- **Code:** `adbCollectionController_v3.ts:1404` watchdog, `:639` startBatch, `:843` stopBatch reconcile.
- **Tables:** `adb_collection_batches`, `adb_collection_subs`, `flight_events`, `raw_airborne_events`, `flight_population` etc.
- **Config:** frozen manifest values only: hard cap `1900`, protected Alert floor `1000`, measured/frozen SOFT_STOP/unsettled-burst controls, exact run ceiling/ledgers, calendar/seeds, and other registry fields. Historical `ADB_MIN_BATCH_CREDITS=300` is non-binding and must be removed/disabled or explicitly proven operational-only without changing the frozen design.
- **Current command contract:** Phase 6 starts **only** through `v39:phase6:start -- --auth <AUTH_ID> --manifest-sha256 <exact_hash>` after FREEZE and separate exact authorization. The watchdog may schedule/continue only within that frozen authorization; preparation remains `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false`. `v39:phase6:pause` and `v39:phase6:status` must be immediately available.
- **API/accounting:** Flight Alert billing is per provider SEND item under the pinned contract; FIDS REST/API-unit cost/range is a separately verified Gate-0/Gate-0.5 account fact and must not be hard-coded from the historical 2-unit observation.
- **Outputs:** 31 solver-defined batch-days plus the realized counts of unique flights, PRE snapshots, POST snapshots and airborne points. Alert-credit spend is **not** a proxy for any of those row/sample counts.
- **Artifacts:** Phase-6 daily records (§21 format).
- **Tests:** scaled soft-stop/hard-cap state machine, second-start guard, unsettled-burst behavior, and delivery_failure pause; production 1900−50=1850 may be asserted as an arithmetic fixture, not a permanently frozen runtime margin.
- **Gate:** none (run itself), but overshoot MISMATCH flags batch.
- **Failure:** HARD_CAP 1900 overshoot → MISMATCH.
- **DoD:** all 31 **batch-days** complete under the frozen solver/calendar and authorization without unresolved `MISMATCH`; every owning budget day settles/reconciles and all stop/failure records are resolved according to the frozen protocol.
- **Next:** Phase7.

#### 4.6.8 Phase 7 — Month-1 deliverables — snapshot materialization/rerun + evaluation

- **Objective:** Early operational pilot, not validated production model.
- **Steps 25-29:** execute/rerun the PRE/AIRBORNE snapshot builders that **must have been** production-wired and offline-tested before Phase 6; materialize actual collected rows, then fit/evaluate Model −1 vs Model 1. Month 1 reports A/B/C/D/R/P + POST; Engine E remains deferred until its event taxonomy/source is frozen.
- **Code:** repository intake must map the verified snapshot/materialization/evaluation owners. Historical `historicalFeatureStore_v3.ts` and earlier evaluation paths are inspection targets only.
- **Tables:** `flight_snapshots` (T-24/6/90), `flight_airborne_snapshots` (t), `flight_outcomes` target-specific labels.
- **Config:** `history_ready_at`, `split_rule_hash` → test row IDs.
- **Commands:** repository intake must expose/map verified post-collection snapshot-materialization and protected pilot-evaluation commands before Phase 6 is authorized. Historical `snapshot-etl` / `eval -- --engine A` names are inspection targets, not assumed current interfaces.
- **Outputs:** Engine-A T-6 result for the frozen `selected_primary_target`, reporting whether Model 1 is better/equal/worse than Model −1 under the preregistered ≥2-min practical threshold + CI rule; staleness/collection-mechanism diagnostics. No winning result is assumed.
- **Tests:** constructible-at-cutoff must error, same-flight POST partition no leak, final-test protection.
- **DoD:** Report `early operational pilot`, GNN deferred.
- **Next:** Month2 power analysis trigger.

**Why the phases are ordered this way (the science):** you prove the *money
math* (Phase 1) before you *measure the world* (Phase 2) before you *prove the
pipeline honestly* (Phase 3–4) before you *freeze the config* (Phase 5) before you
*spend* (Phase 6). Every gate protects the next one from a silent invalidation.
Sources backing this: the credit/retry rules come from AeroDataBox's own Flight
Alert guide; stratification is standard survey design; the "persistence first" gate
comes from Chen & Li 2019 and Sternberg 2017 (see Plan §19).

### 4.7 Gate-by-gate guide — A30 §34 (16 fields each, preserves rl8/rl9 history)

#### 4.7.1 GATE-0 — Budget partition (Plan §16 Gate0, A30 §34)

- **Purpose:** Verify money math before measuring world.
- **Sci rationale:** 58,900 vs 57,900 vs 1,000 floor confusion caused legacy 1,100 error; refill 1:1 must be live-verified.
- **Ops rationale:** FIDS REST cost must stay on the API-unit ledger, not the Alert-credit envelope. The Sep-2 document audit carried a last-observed public FIDS tier/cost pin, but Gate 0 must re-verify the actual current provider/account endpoint cost/range and freeze that evidence.
- **Prereq:** current offline P0/P1 implementation/test closure passed for every Gate-0 dependency; `DATABASE_URL` + `AERODATABOX_API_KEY` available; `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false`. Historical “Phase0 DONE” labels do not satisfy this prerequisite.
- **Code path:** the repository intake must map the production Gate-0/account-budget owner. Historical `scripts/gate0_budget_report.ts` is only a last-known inspection target; no current existence/wiring claim is made.
- **Current command contract:** `v39:gate0:inspect -- --auth <AUTH_ID> --evidence-id <GATE0_ID>` after repository mapping. External/account reads are governed by an exact authorization because cost/tier can change; if the mapped operation is proven free, the authorization records a zero spend ceiling rather than removing the guard.
- **Provider/account:** RapidAPI plan monthly units, per-refill cap, balance cap.
- **Inputs:** USER-CONFIRMED `MONTHLY_PLAN_ENTITLEMENT_UNITS=60000`, plus live verification that the same subscribed plan remains active, current cycle dates/remaining units/usage, and `AERODATABOX_API_KEY`. Do not infer the marketplace/channel name from the entitlement alone.
- **Expected output:** current settled Alert balance + billing-cycle/API entitlement + refill conversion + protected floor/pre-run/Phase6/ending allocations and generated REST category caps. PASS requires the exact identities to balance; historical `2900`/`57,900` values are evidence/context, not assumed current values.
- **PASS:** actual plan/billing cycle/refill conversion verified; exact Alert and API identities balance; generated REST category total fits its protected allocation. Prior `939<1000` estimate alone is insufficient.
- **FAIL:** any unresolved account identity, billing-cycle, cost/tier, refill-conversion, cap, floor, or two-ledger identity is fail-closed. Diagnose from the new evidence artifact; do not rely on historical “expected FAIL while idle” output.
- **Historical output only:** rl3 observed 862→863 (+1 refill conversion) and rl9 later observed balance 2900. These are dated provenance, **not** a current Gate-0 PASS or current balance.
- **Artifacts:** `rl3.md`, `manifest:budget_partition`.
- **Hashes:** `gate0_report_hash`.
- **Cost:** live Gate-0 verification must use the minimum explicitly authorized provider/account action needed to verify conversion/caps; do not hard-code a mandatory 1-credit refill if the current provider/account can be verified another way. Record any actual units/credits consumed.
- **Warnings/remediation:** record the exact current mismatch and repair only that dependency. Historical placeholders/reserve values/login advice are provenance, not a generic remediation recipe.
- **Rerun:** only the mapped, authorization-guarded `v39:gate0:inspect` path, with a new evidence record whenever freshness or account/provider state requires re-verification.
- **Final status:** **BLOCKED_LIVE_EVIDENCE** for the current run. Historical 2026-08-16/19 evidence is preserved but does not freeze present account/billing values.
- **Freeze:** `budget_partition` + `spendable_envelope` in manifest.
- **Next permitted:** `PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION` verification; Gate 1 only after that passes.
- **Prohibited:** any billable/retained FIDS acquisition before Gate 0 and the applicable security/retention authorization path permit it; Gate-1 coverage/reference acquisition follows the current sequence in §1.

#### 4.7.2 GATE-1 — Coverage (Plan §16 Gate1)

- **Purpose:** Measure universe, not assume 276.
- **Prereq:** Gate 0 PASS **and** `PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION=PASS` for any content Gate 1 will create/store.
- **Code:** repository intake must map the actual provider-coverage acquisition and local aggregation owners. Historical `computeAirportCoverage` / `scripts/measure_coverage.ts` paths are last-known inspection targets only.
- **Current commands:** `v39:gate1:coverage -- --auth <AUTH_ID> --evidence-id <GATE1_ID>` acquires/hashes current provider-backed coverage; `v39:reference:freeze` freezes the licensed traffic/region metric/cut/mapping/normalization inputs; `v39:frame:build` rebuilds/hashes the final frame; only **after that** does `v39:preprobe:freeze` freeze the exact 12-candidate shortlist, ordered replacements, yield-reference rules and probe protocol bound to the frame hash. Legacy `coverage` is an inspection target only.
- **Expected output:** a freshly measured provider-backed universe artifact with source endpoints, retrieval timestamp, provider/OpenAPI version, response hashes, local-aggregator version/hash if used, current `universeCount`, current catalog intersection, and eligibility counts. No old count is the target.
- **PASS:** measurement succeeds from the currently verified provider coverage inputs with recorded endpoint/tier/cost/version/hash, identity/mapping QC passes, and the sanity relationship is evaluated on the **current** artifact; any catalog comparison is diagnostic, not proof that old counts must repeat.
- **Historical actual:** `rl7` observed 4,332 universe / 267 catalog-in-universe on 2026-08-17 through the legacy local route; provenance only.
- **Artifacts:** new current Gate-1 artifact + hashes; retain `rl7.md` as historical provenance.
- **Cost:** the Sep-2 evidence treated the relevant health/coverage inputs as non-paid/free-tier; **re-verify current provider cost/tier before execution and record any API-unit cost**.
- **Status:** **BLOCKED** until current authorized coverage acquisition, security/retention, reference freeze inputs, and hashes are evidenced.

#### 4.7.3 GATE-2 — Anchor probe (Plan §16 Gate2, §9)

- **Purpose:** Freeze anchor pool via measured yield, not fame.
- **Prereq:** current Gate 0 and Gate 1 PASS; frozen traffic/region/reference rules + rebuilt frame + hash-locked pre-probe freeze record; mandatory tiny pre-probe safety smoke PASS; corrected probe code/offline tests PASS.
- **Code:** repository intake must map the production probe/scoring owner and prove that it implements the current Plan formulas/censoring/identity/settlement rules. Historical `scripts/anchor_probe.ts` is a last-known candidate, not current proof.
- **Command contract:** sequential frozen-list Stage 1 target-2h probes, then five valid target-4h confirmations; both stages cap-censored against the owning `probe_budget_day_id` with `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500`; no UTC-midnight reset. BLOCKED pending frozen lists, code correction, and human approval.
- **PASS:** scores 0-1, capacity gate applied, pool NOT locked before measurement.
- **Historical actual:** rl8 produced 0 deliveries, which did **not** establish whether reachability or activation was the cause; rl9 later proved reachability but the handler failed before storage because of the historical `is_randomized` bug. Neither run is a current Gate-2 result.
- **Status:** BLOCKED pending offline probe correctness, current frame/pre-probe freeze, Gate0/1, explicit paid-action authorization, and the mandatory tiny pre-probe safety smoke. Official Gate 3 is later.

#### 4.7.4 GATE-3 — Credit canary (Plan §16 Gate3, §11.2)

- **Purpose:** End-to-end accounting proof.
- **Code:** repository intake must identify/map the production canary owner; historical `scripts/credit_canary.ts` is only a last-known path candidate. The implementation must use R1 exclusivity plus the shared settlement rule.
- **Current command contract:** `npm run v39:gate3:canary -- --auth <AUTH_ID> --evidence-id <GATE_ID>` after §2 mapping exists. Cost/duration are bounded by the exact authorization and are **not** assumed to be one credit or a fixed number of minutes.
- **PASS:** `C_external == C_internal` (`CANARY_TOLERANCE=0`), `failures=0`, >0 items, raw-before-2xx proof, confirmed cleanup/no foreign billable subscription, and **at least three consecutive equal authoritative balance reads across the frozen stability window** (or a larger frozen count).
- **Historical actual:** rl9 FAIL `C_ext=1 C_int=0 failures=1`; a later code change was reported for the `is_randomized` NULL defect. Current repository presence, wiring, regression tests, and live behavior are **NOT_VERIFIED**.
- **Artifacts:** `rl9.md` is dated provenance only.
- **Status:** **BLOCKED**; a new current canary PASS is required after all predecessors.

#### 4.7.5 GATE-0.5 — Dedicated provider/content/clock/T/target pilot (Plan §16 Gate0.5)

- **Purpose:** Prove payload honest before spending.
- **Prereq:** Gate 3 PASS, the Gate-0.5 adequacy thresholds frozen before observing pilot results, and an exact pilot authorization.
- **Code:** repository intake must map the current webhook/raw-ingress, milestone/clock, trajectory, target/terminalizer, and pilot-analysis owners. Historical paths are inspection targets only.
- **Expected:** raw event preserves provider/source facts, `event_phase`, `data_stage`, complete canonical Plan §6.4 clock taxonomy, optional semantic `event_timestamp` (nullable), and live-location observations; `prediction_state` exists only on snapshots. Movement semantics/actuality, T constructibility, trajectories, and cadence are measured.
- **PASS:** all frozen minimum sample counts/duration are met **and** the semantic/durability/leakage/trajectory checks pass. If checks do not fail but sample minimums are unmet, result=`INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`, never PASS.
- **Actual:** PENDING (canary FAIL blocked).
- **Freeze:** `grace_minutes`, `airborne_usable_min`, `cadence thresholds` (Plan §6.6).
- **Status:** BLOCKED.

#### 4.7.6 GATE-4 — Webhook + cap (Plan §16 Gate4, §11.3)

- **Code:** repository intake must map the production watchdog/ledger owner. Offline tests use injectable cap=100/margin=10 and separately assert the production arithmetic fixture; the runtime margin is whatever the smoke/canary protocol validly freezes, not an unreviewed historical constant.
- **PASS:** scaled offline cap/failure-state tests pass first; the small authorized live check then reconciles exactly, preserves `maxDeliveryRetries=0`, refuses a second start while unsettled/unauthorized, and does not deliberately spend toward the 1,900 ceiling merely to test it.
- **Actual:** PENDING.
- **Status:** BLOCKED.

#### 4.7.7 GATE-5 — Population/census (Plan §16 Gate5, §5)

- **Code:** standalone `fidsCensus_v3.ts:fetchFidsPopulation`; direction `Both`, but interval is the exact service window split to the live-verified max range, never a fixed 12h. LAST_KNOWN_CODED/UNIT_TESTED; current file, test, and production-wiring status = NOT_VERIFIED until repository intake.
- **PASS:** `captured_in_population <= population_total`; `captured_outside_population` is reported/investigated separately, with expected/created snapshots and observed/missing outcomes quantified.
- **Actual:** NOT PASSED — a FIDS helper was present in the last-observed repository, but current file existence, production wiring, guard/boundary/selector evidence, tests, and live Gate-5 execution are unverified.
- **Status:** BLOCKED.

#### 4.7.8 FREEZE — manifest (Plan §17 Phase5, §13.2-13.6)

- **Code:** repository intake must map the manifest writer; current operator interface is `v39:freeze` after all prerequisite evidence passes.
- **Inputs:** every current V3.9-f.8 required frozen/MEASURE→FREEZE value, the unchanged hash-locked `preprobe_reference_freeze_record`, exact two-ledger budgets, solver/calendar hash, retention/security/history/weather readiness, canonical registry/traceability, and `split_rule_hash` (not precollection test row IDs).
- **PASS:** manifest is complete and hash-stable; all prerequisite evidence is PASS; `v39:scanner` and `v39:preflight` pass with zero unresolved current P0/P1 document/repository contradictions; no required registry/traceability/budget/retention value is unknown; split/calendar/preprobe hashes are embedded.
- **Status:** **BLOCKED / NOT FROZEN**.

#### 4.7.9 FINAL PREFLIGHT — lexical scan

- **Current interface:** `v39:scanner` + `v39:preflight` perform the context-aware Plan/Log/repository scan. Raw lexical grep may be used as a diagnostic only; it cannot be the sole closure proof.
- **Status:** BLOCKED.

<a id="sec-7"></a>
## 7. GO/NO-GO verdict and mandatory handoff checkpoints

The supplied Run Reports demonstrate real implementation progress, so the old “repository unavailable” verdict is superseded. They do **not** demonstrate Phase-0 closure.

```text
DOCUMENT_PACKAGE = ALIGNED_AT_DOCUMENT_SCOPE
REPOSITORY_PROGRESS = REAL_EVIDENCE_PRESENT
PHASE0_OFFLINE_CLOSURE = NO-GO / INCOMPLETE
GATE0 = BLOCKED (dated read-only provenance exists)
PAID_SAFETY_SMOKE = NO-GO
ANCHOR_PROBES = NO-GO
PHASE6 = NO-GO
CURRENT_ACTION = FINISH_PHASE0_OFFLINE_CLOSURE
MANDATORY_RETURN = AFTER_FULL_PHASE0_PREFLIGHT_BEFORE_FURTHER_GATE0_PROGRESS
```

### 7.1 Why the Run Report's “next = complete Gate 0” is not the current controlling next step

The report correctly records useful Gate-0 reads, but it also records missing TEST IDs and 57 full-repo typecheck errors. Under §5/§18 and Plan §16–§17, offline closure precedes Gate 0. Therefore:

- preserve the earlier Gate-0 observations as evidence;
- do not erase or rewrite the immutable run entries;
- treat the report's old “What's Next = Gate 0” text as **superseded advisory guidance**;
- current next step is §1.3 Phase-0 closure and §2.6 aggregate verification.

### 7.2 First mandatory return point

Bring back the updated Run Reports when the current repository can show, on one recorded evidence scope:

1. current branch/HEAD/worktree;
2. TEST-001–028 required offline portions closed;
3. migration fresh/upgrade/repeat/constraint/recovery proof;
4. complete discovered test suite PASS;
5. typecheck 0;
6. lint PASS or explicitly adjudicated blocker;
7. production build PASS;
8. registry/traceability/dictionary/lineage closure;
9. scanner PASS;
10. aggregate `v39:preflight` PASS;
11. artifact hashes and unresolved warnings.

Then STOP before further account/provider action and return for Gate-0 adjudication.

### 7.3 Later mandatory return points

The definitive schedule is §1.12: after smoke; after material Stage-1 decisions; after Gate 2; after each Gate 3/0.5/4/5; after FREEZE; and immediately on any Phase-6 pause/failure/mismatch.

## 8. Decision record and implementation priority queue

**DEC-001 — No V3.10:** selected patch f.7+f.8; rejected V3.10 (violates absolute versioning).

### 8.1 Dependency-aware implementation priority queue (derived from binding Plan; not proof of code status)

Severity and dependency are both considered. A lower-numbered item may block many later items even when another item is equally P0.

**P0 — resolve/code/test before any paid collection:**
1. retention-rights gate + configurable compliant raw lifecycle; normalized raw ingress before 2xx;
2. FIDS population query roles/timezone/range/scope/codeshare provenance, **record-class timestamp applicability, internal half-open boundary filtering, and frozen `population_acquisition_guard_minutes` + `population_refresh_cadence_minutes` + `population_max_staleness_minutes`**;
3. stable flight-instance/retime/codeshare identities;
4. canonical clocks + `available_at`/`materialized_at` semantics;
5. provider milestone/T/actual-vs-estimated constructibility guards;
6. historical/weather as-known-at-cutoff stores and leakage tests;
7. PRE and AIRBORNE snapshot builders with population-defined row existence;
8. target-specific terminalizer/outcome-recovery state machine with generated/coalesced `OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET`;
9. SEND-aware Alert accounting, exact canary, hard-cap/soft-stop safety;
10. 31-day SAT calendar with exact five crossover pairs/split containment + separate Alert-credit/REST-unit budget proof.

**P1 — resolve before final frame/FREEZE/Phase 6:** traffic/region reference classification and final frame; anchor probe/reference exactness; production `m_i`/zero-yield integration; split/test protection and Engine-E deferral; complete registry/requirement matrix/schema dictionary/lineage/contradiction scanner; migration/typecheck/integration closure.

**P2 — complete before publication/strong inference:** diagnostic/report reproducibility, staleness/ablation/MV presentation, analysis metadata completeness, archive cleanup where it cannot affect executable authority.

**P3 — cosmetic/history:** wording or organization that cannot change denominator, billing, leakage, sampling, labels, identity, or executable code behavior.

A live/provider/human blocker is tracked separately from priority; it must not be relabeled as an offline implementation PASS.

## 10. Database data dictionary — required current contract

**Every table:** `table name / purpose / producer / consumer / PK / unique keys / FKs / indexes / row unit / cardinality / append-only vs mutable / source of truth / retention / provenance`

**Every column:** `name / SQL type / nullable / unit / semantics / source: provider/derived/our server/reference / timestamp type / availability rule / producer / consumers / missing-state meaning / QC`

The table below mixes existing legacy objects, migration-created-but-unapplied objects, and intended objects. It is not proof that every row/column exists or is production-wired. A complete schema-introspection-backed column dictionary is `NOT_VERIFIED` in this document pass and remains mandatory repository work; any intended object is DOCUMENTED unless separately evidenced as migration-created, tested, applied, and wired. **Retention is a data-class decision, not a table-name shortcut. Classify every important table/column as `raw_api_content`, `derived_work_candidate/derived_work`, `non_aerodatabox_project_metadata`, or another sourced class with legal basis. AeroDataBox Article 5.5 governs retained raw API Contents unless Plan Terms grant another period; Article 5.6 states that §5.5 does not apply to Derived Works, but other Terms restrictions still do. Therefore no table below receives an unconditional “permanent” claim merely because it is normalized or useful.**

| Table | Purpose | Producer | Consumer | PK | Unique | FKs | Indexes | Row unit | Cardinality | Append/mutable | Source of truth | Retention | Provenance |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `clean.adb_sampling_frame` | measured/frozen airport frame (current count not hard-coded) | `build_stratified_catalog.ts` corrected path | `adbCollectionController:pickAirportCandidates` | `icao` | `icao` | none | `tier,region,pre_eligible,post_eligible` | airport | measured at current rebuild | mutable until FREEZE then read-only | provider coverage + frozen reference hashes | **non-AeroDataBox project metadata / reference-derived fields; retention per project/reference-source policy, provided raw provider Contents are not embedded** | `coverage_hash` `tier_version` `region_mapping_hash` |
